## Part 1. METHODS

## METHODS, group 1: FT1 timing, FT2 labels, FT3 Pulse base, F1 funnel, FT5 doctors, FT8 history
Compiled 20 Oct 2026 (IST) by a read-only appendix helper (Opus 5.5) from lane files only; nothing was run. sha = sha256[:12], computed 20 Oct.
Shared conventions used by every lane below (from ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/SWARM.md "forest-lead data notes", 19 Oct 16:40):
- Outcomes: gating-lead f1-s5 monthly exports from Metabase db13 (OT notes, Surgery/IP-Package bills, IP admissions; window Rx day to +90 d). Cleaned copies: ~/eta-data/forest/funnel/s5-clean/ (1,938 records). Raw copies: ~/wt/forest-funnel-join/.bc/out/f1-s5-*.tsv.
- Label rules: convert-lead p3a_counts.py (sha 490f5dc17bd3), exclusions R1 (obstetric) > R4 (staged/second procedure) > R2 (same-day minor). Event = min(first OT, first bill). Episode = (RD-0 cluster key, event date), credited to the earliest flagged Rx. Cluster with no event = one negative episode at its first flagged Rx.
- Maturity: H14 final at Rx age >= 18 d (14 + 4 d billing lag); H90 final at >= 94 d. Read date 2026-10-19.
- Split: RD-0 lock RD0-2026-10-18-52959278edd8 (list pin 52959278edd8, seed pin 3b10cbf1f278) via split_guard.py (~/oc/forest-lead/ft/vendor_split/split_guard.py, 2d39ef2884ee). H is sealed. FT runs on D only. FT1 is the only exception: it pools D+H counts and fits no model.
- Cohort keys: ~/eta-data/forest/split-input/10.tsv (prescription_uid to patient_key), clusters-2026-10-18.tsv. Specialty: ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/doctor_specialty.tsv (019103f11334; 89 doctors, derived from ft1a).
- Python: ~/.venv-forest (Python 3.10.12, scikit-learn 1.7.2, statsmodels 0.15.0, scipy 1.15.3, pandas 2.3.3, numpy 2.2.6; versions read 20 Oct, not pinned in any lane file).
- Export reader: ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/ftio.py read_export (4486d8d2b96e). It rebuilds records broken by unescaped newlines.

### FT1 timing (tags S1, S19)
- Collection
  - Inputs:
    - f1-s5 db13 outcome exports, raw copies in ~/wt/forest-funnel-join/.bc/out/f1-s5-*.tsv (flagged Rx from 2025-12-01);
    - 10.tsv; clusters-2026-10-18.tsv; doctor_specialty.tsv.
  - Rx window used: 18 Dec 2025 - 17 Jul 2026 (Rx age >= 94 d at the read date, 19 Oct).
  - Lane: forest-lead. Script written 19 Oct 16:28; FT1 marked DONE 16:33 (SWARM.md). The FT7 Sources table says "stdout counts, run 19 Oct". No log file is kept.
  - Refute: ft-refute-ft1, 20 Oct 03:59-04:03 IST.
- Cut-off and leakage rules
  - Counts only; no model is fitted, so D and H are pooled (FT7 section 2).
  - Matured = Rx >= 94 d old at READ 2026-10-19.
  - No explicit lag <= 90 filter. The maximum observed lag is 82 d (refute fix 4).
  - The refuter read no H field beyond ids, dates, specialty and the label (prompt rule).
- Processing
  - Records are rebuilt by uid start, as in ftio.
  - Rx before 2025-12-01 are dropped. The Rx is mapped to patient_key (10.tsv) and then to its cluster.
  - Event = min(first_ot_date, first_bill_date). R1/R4/R2 exclusions are applied as listed in the shared conventions.
  - Positive episode = first Rx per (cluster, event date).
  - Cumulative share by day 0/3/7/14/21/30/60/90, overall and by specialty of the anchor Rx's doctor.
  - Median = lags[n//2], the upper median. This gives the Urology bug (7 printed, true median 6).
- Engines / models
  - Python stdlib only; no ML.
  - Serving LLM of forest-lead: not stated in the FT1 files. Elsewhere it is recorded as "forest-lead (claude-opus-5-5)" (~/wt/forest-rl/.bc/RL-SPEC.md).
  - Refuter: Opus 5.5, global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5, Bedrock (report line 1).
- Checks
  - The refuter wrote its own code (work/ft1_own.py) on s5-clean, with p3a sha 490f5dc17bd3 asserted.
  - Its numbers were frozen in work/OWN-NUMBERS.txt before it read ft1_timing.py.
  - Every cell reproduces except the Urology median. 0 row mismatches against labels.parquet on 1,622/1,622 rows.
  - Rx-to-episode reconciliation: 978 Rx to 883 episodes.
  - Wilson CIs for d14; Fisher test GS vs Ortho+Gyn, p 0.18.
  - Verdict PASS-WITH-FIXES (6 fixes).
- Key files
  - ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/ft1_timing.py 3b36eb5d076b
  - ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/ftio.py 4486d8d2b96e
  - ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/f1_recompute.py ab5c52daa554 (forest-lead's F1 recompute, which exec's ft1_timing.py)

### FT2 labels (tags S2, S4)
- Collection
  - Inputs:
    - raw f1-s5 exports (forest-funnel-join .bc/out);
    - 10.tsv (prescription_uid to patient_key, and consult_uid in v2);
    - clusters-2026-10-18.tsv; lock-2026-10-18.tsv; doctor_specialty.tsv.
  - Rx from 2025-12-01; read date 2026-10-19.
  - Lane: ft-labels-base. v1 was written 16:41 IST (sha 28cecac9ef55). v2 was written 16:55 (sha 0aefc00f425b; labels.DONE at 16:57).
  - Refute: ft-refute-labels, 19 Oct 16:45-16:48. It targeted v1 (28cecac9ef55).
- Cut-off and leakage rules
  - v2 applies forest-lead's ruling: h14 is NaN when Rx age < 18 d (v1 used < 14 d, which was refute issue 1); h90 is NaN when < 94 d.
  - H rows are labelled only. No features are read for them.
- Processing
  - ft2_labels.py exec's the ft1_timing.py header (regexes, pk, cl, spec), so the episode logic is shared with FT1.
  - Rows are sorted by (rx date, consult time, prescription_uid).
  - episode_id = sha256(cluster|event)[:16].
  - Output: 1,622 episodes (D 1,233 / H 389) from 1,909 Rx. D h14 1,101/189; D h90 659/150.
- Engines / models
  - pandas/numpy; no ML.
  - Lane LLM: Opus 5.5, global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5, Bedrock (DONE.md line 1; labels.DONE).
  - Refuter: Opus 5.5, Bedrock (report line 1; exact id not stated in the report).
- Checks

  - It read s5-clean, ran split_guard with the pins (seed sha matched) and checked the p3a sha.
  - Every claimed count PASSes (15 rows).
  - Row join on prescription_uid: 0 mismatches on all label columns. 29 duplicate S5 rows were deduped.
  - Verdict PASS-WITH-FIXES (h14 lag rule). The fix is applied in v2.
  - No refute report targets v2 itself. Its D rows are verified by the FT3 and FT5 refuters (0 mismatches on 1,233 D rows).

### FT3 Pulse baseline (tags S3, S5)
- Collection
  - Inputs:
    - labels.parquet v2 (D only);
    - raw f1-s5 (flag_proc_names);
    - 10.tsv and clusters (prior flagged Rx);
    - data/ft5a.tsv, ft3b.tsv, ft3c.tsv, ft3d.tsv (gating-lead pulls of 19 Oct).
  - Lane: ft-labels-base. Code written 19 Oct 17:11-17:12. The final run wrote ft3_results.json at 17:35; DONE.md at 17:36.
  - Refute: ft-refute-base, 17:37-18:06.
- Cut-off and leakage rules
  - D only (features_D.parquet asserts split == 'D').
  - Same-day payer (ft5a payer_appt/payer_bill) is excluded by default: the COLS filter drops it unless PAYER=1. The leaky run is kept as ft3_results_withpayer.json.
  - Prior-visit payer (ft3d) = last appointment strictly before the Rx day.
  - n_prior_flagged = flagged Rx in the cluster strictly before the anchor consult time.
  - Doctor rate: smoothed (m=10) and in-fold. Training rows get an inner 5-fold GroupKFold out-of-fold rate; test rows get the train-only rate.
  - Aug-Sep D Rx are used for the H14 temporal test only, never for H90 (seal note).
- Processing
  - Features: specialty; doctor rate and volume; procedure class (15 regex classes on flagged names); n_procs; month_idx; n_prior_flagged; age; sex; n_prior_rx; ICD count, missing flag and chapter counts; investigations count and missing flag; doctor gender; payer_prior; days_since_prior_appt.
  - One-hot levels with n >= 10.
  - Temporal split: train Dec-May (n 441); test Jun-Sep for H14, 1 Jun-17 Jul for H90.
  - CV: StratifiedGroupKFold, 5 folds x 5 repeats, groups = RD-0 cluster.
  - 1,000-draw cluster bootstrap for CIs; permutation importance by feature group.
- Engines / models
  - sklearn RandomForestClassifier: n_estimators 400, min_samples_leaf 5, max_features 'sqrt', random_state 20261019.
  - No search; no calibration (raw RF probabilities).
  - Lane LLM: global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5 (Bedrock).
  - Refuter: global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5. Its own RF: 500 trees, leaf 5, 5 seeds, cluster bootstrap B=2000, plus an in-fold sigmoid check.
- Checks
  - The refuter built independent labels (a copy of ft-refute-labels recompute.py with the 18 d rule), its own features and its own models.
  - Numbers were written down before it read the lane code.
  - forest-lead checks (1) to (4) all PASS: Aug-Sep use, payer, Brier/leakage, within-specialty CIs.
  - Verdict PASS-WITH-FIXES (6 issues). FT7 quotes the refuter's figures in brackets.
  - Three runs were scored on the same Jun-Sep test (withpayer 0.650, nopayer 0.600, final 0.597); this is disclosed.
- Key files

### F1 funnel (tag S16)
- Collection
  - Sources:
    - S1/S5: f1-s5-2025-12..2026-10.sql (db13, 11 monthly chunks in the convert-lead 07b shape);
    - S2/S3: f1-s23-s4.sql (db16 Runo leads/calls plus db16 lead-status fields);
    - S4 primary: f1-s4-pulse-booking.sql (db "Firestore to Postgres (prod)", Pulse surgery_cases);
    - shared-phone key list: f1-10e2-keys.sql (db13);
    - S4 coverage dates: 11a.
  - SQL written by the funnel-join builder (19 Oct 06:18-06:43) and run by gating-lead.
  - Code: ~/wt/forest-funnel-join/studies/2026-10-19-funnel/funnel_f1.py, branch forest/funnel-join, HEAD 524a77a3 (R4, 19 Oct 11:54; worktree clean for that dir).
  - Real-data run: by forest-lead after the Opus refute PASS (forest-funnel-join-R2.md: "forest-lead runs F1 after an Opus PASS"). F1-COUNTS.md was written 19 Oct 16:31; SWARM.md says "FUNNEL F1 (done 16:32)".
  - Run arguments: read date 2026-10-19, S5 cut 2026-10-19, lock RD0-2026-10-18-52959278edd8, --s4-from 2026-06-26, --s4-rx-from 2026-09-25. The exact command line is not stored.
  - Per-episode output: ~/eta-data/forest/funnel/hash-only/f1-2026-10-19.tsv.
- Cut-off and leakage rules
  - Each rate is computed within one split and never pooled. H is descriptive only.
  - S5 denominator = Rx >= 94 d old. Pending Rx are never counted as losses.
  - S4 window = Rx day to +90 d. An open window = S4-pending.
  - Credit-cluster rule: a link counts for the latest episode Rx on or before the link, inside the window, on or before the S5 event, once.
  - Ambiguous episodes (shared contact_hash or 10e2 shared phone) are counted in S1/S5 but kept out of the S2/S3/S4 rates.
  - Pulse PRESCRIBED/CANCELLED cases are not bookings. The Daily Dash API is never called.
- Processing
  - split_guard is loaded with the pins. p3a_counts.py is imported (sha-checked); only its grouping is re-keyed from patient_grp to the RD-0 cluster.
  - Rates start 2026-01 (Dec 2025 has no leads).
  - Outputs: F1-COUNTS.md (counts only) and an admin-only by-doctor file.
- Engines / models
  - Python stdlib; no ML.
  - Builder: claude-sonnet-5-5 (funnel-join.done; R4.done).
  - Refuters: Opus 5.5 (funnel-join-r1-oref, -r3-oref reports).
  - Runner forest-lead: see FT1 (claude-opus-5-5 per RL-SPEC.md).
  - FT7 section 3 cites an "Opus recompute matched exactly": ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/f1_recompute.py (forest-lead, 16:32). Its output is not stored.
- Checks
  - R4: 100 pytest passed (synthetic fixtures), split_guard 65/65, mutants M07/M08/M11 killed.
  - r1-oref: PASS-WITH-FIXES on 31/31. r3-oref: PASS-WITH-FIXES, 75/75.
  - Gates in ~/oc/forest-lead/funnel/gates/ are for T1/T2 call/transcript tagging, not F1 counts.

### FT5 doctors (tags S6, S7, S18)
- Collection
  - Inputs:
    - labels.parquet v2 (D);
    - data/ft5a.tsv (ft5a-casemix.sql), ft3b.tsv, ft1a.tsv (names for the admin report);
    - doctor_specialty.tsv;
    - pitch candidates: ft6-candidates.txt (83) plus convert-lead p2v2/rf775 Haiku f01 >= 0.5 (62 D);
    - 10.tsv + 10b.tsv for the split check.
  - Lane: ft-doctors. v2 at 17:05; v3 (seal fix) DONE at 19 Oct 17:50-17:57.
  - Refutes (two sets, both titled ft-refute-doctors, Opus 5.5):
    - ~/wt/ft-refute-doctors/.bc/ft-refute-doctors-REPORT.md, 17:42;
    - ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/out/ft-refute-doctors/VERDICT.md, 17:55. FT7 section 6 numbers come from this file.
    - Whether both came from the same agent session is not stated.
  - Derm billing pull: ftref-derm-billtypes.sql, run by gating-lead (19 Oct 17:30-17:33). Aggregate only, D+H combined.
- Cut-off and leakage rules
  - D only. Every pitch consult goes through split_guard (consult to patient_key via 10.tsv + 10b.tsv, then cluster, then lock). v2 used 10.tsv only and leaked 15 H consults (refute fix 1); v3 keeps 145 D and drops 35 H.
  - Same-day payer is used as case mix here (FT5 is not a predictive model). ft3d's prior payer is not used.
  - Eligibility: doctor with >= 10 matured episodes; specialty with >= 2 such doctors and >= 1 conversion. This drops Dermatology.
- Processing
  - ft5.py builds episodes_D.pkl from labels.
  - ft5_analysis.py computes:
    - Wilson CIs;
    - case-mix logistic GLM, giving expected counts and O/E with a Byar CI;
    - one-way ANOVA ICC;
    - ML GLMM with 40-node Gauss-Hermite and a profile CI;
    - VB GLMM (statsmodels BinomialBayesMixedGLM, vcp_p=1);
    - fixed-effect LR;
    - 5x5 CV AUC gain;
    - specialty volumes and lost-conversion scenarios (written to specialty_volume.tsv);
    - pairs.
  - ft5_tables.py renders FT5-REPORT.md (admin-only; contains names).
- Engines / models
  - statsmodels, scipy, sklearn.
  - Lane LLM: global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5 (DONE.md).
  - Refuter (VERDICT.md): global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5. Own ML GLMM (25-node GH, analytic gradients, profile CI, 200-replicate parametric bootstrap, boundary LR 0.5·chi2_1). Case-mix sets "lite" and "full". VB cross-check with vcp_p 1 and 0.5.
  - Refuter (wt REPORT): Opus 5.5 (Bedrock). Adaptive GH-30 ML GLMM, VB, 5x20 CV.
- Checks
  - wt refuter: numbers frozen in work/MY-NUMBERS.md before it read the lane outputs. labels v2 matches 1,233/1,233 D rows. Cohort 833/163/18/4 and 425/125/12/3 PASS. Found the seal leak (15 H ids). Verdict PASS-WITH-FIXES.
  - VERDICT.md: PASS-WITH-FIXES, 5 fixes, all applied per FT7: H ids removed, Ortho pair dropped, frequentist CI, GS contrast downgraded, Derm cause stated.
  - Correcting for 18 doctors moves the GS low doctor's p from 0.013-0.022 to 0.23-0.40.

## METHODS, group 2: recorded consults (FT4), call text (FTC), calls-predict-conversion (FTR), Runo audio + Sarvam STT (RL-1..3), call-audio features (ft-rl-feat)
Compiled 20 Oct 2026 (IST) by a read-only helper from lane DONE/REPORT/COVERAGE files, .bc prompts and claims, and code headers. sha256 = first 12 hex chars, computed 20 Oct.
None of the lane dirs are git repos. Code refs are `sha:<sha256[:12]>` of the script, except the gated RL repo (git).

Sha drift against the FT7 Sources table (read 20 Oct 04:00):
- S10 ft-calltext/DONE.md is now 734fbdd0eaad; Sources has 718702d57880. The file was rewritten at 04:13 when the FTR final v2 section was added.
- S13 ft-refute-ftr-final-REPORT.md is now 1666413a466a; Sources has 743ec712e02b. A "v2 delta" section was appended at 04:35.
- Every other S8-S17 file in this group matches Sources.

### M-FT4. Recorded consults (lanes ft-audio and ft-cohortb-expand; refutes ft-refute-audio and ft-refute-audio2) [S8, S9]
**Collection**
- Audio and engine outputs came from the consult clip index (`~/eta-data/consult/clips`, consult.flac) and the REB track store (`~/eta-data/reb/<date>/<room>/_consults/`). Consults were recorded from 9 Sep 2026; the expand lane covers 9 Sep-19 Oct.
- Cohort sources:
  - v1: ft6-candidates (83) plus RF775 Haiku-confirmed members (52) = 135 consults, 125 of them D.
  - ft-cohortb-expand: every recorded consult that anchors a D flagged-Rx episode in labels.parquet v2. That is 137 anchors; 88 are new relative to ft-audio.
  - v2 union (ale.py): 137 anchors plus 76 ft-audio non-anchors = 213 consults, or 198 patients at one row per patient.
- Outcomes:
  - Anchors use labels.parquet v2 (0aefc00f425b).
  - Non-anchors use the ft4a pull: `~/oc/forest-lead/sql/ft4a-recorded.sql`, db 13, read-only, 30 d outcome horizon. The SQL header says "Run by gating-lead". Output `~/oc/forest-lead/data/ft4a.tsv` (written 19 Oct 16:54). The lane read it on 19 Oct.
  - Discrepancy: DONE.md cites the ft4a sha as c7021cd608e0. That is the sha of the .sql file; ft4a.tsv hashes to 825f88cfe428.
- Lanes and dates:
  - ft-audio built v1 and v2 on 19 Oct (v1 17:13; v2 DONE 18:43).
  - ft-cohortb-expand built the expansion on 19 Oct (features 17:28; Ale-label update 18:17).
  - The refutes ran on 19 Oct: v1 17:12-18:03, v2 19:00-19:24.

**Cut-off and leakage rules**
- D split only. Every row goes through split_guard (pinned list 52959278edd8; seed 3b10cbf1f278) and fails closed. H rows are dropped unread.
- Label read date: 19 Oct 2026. Labels:
  - Ale (primary): 1 = attributable surgery after the consult, at any age (OT note or Surgery/IP bill, minus R1/R2/R4). 0 = consult at least 14 d old with no surgery. Younger consults with no surgery are dropped.
  - h14: consult at least 18 d old.
  - Ale >= 14 d is the age-bias check.
  - h90 cannot be computed yet (no consult is 94 d old before about 12 Dec).
- Strict episode credit: one row per patient, taken from the earliest cohort consult.
- Leakage handling:
  - Consult age, date and pipeline-source flags are never model features. Age alone gives AUC 0.675 under the Ale label.
  - Jev f07 (a date, slot or booking step given in the room) is flagged as an outcome proxy (LEAKAGE_f07.md) and kept out of the headline.
  - The doctor prior H14 rate is time-respecting.
  - payer and val_change were dropped from the primary feature sets because their fill was under 60% (refute fix 1).

**Processing steps**
- Feature table:
  - ft-audio: inv.py, base.py, assemble.py, audio_feats.py.
  - ft-cohortb-expand: inventory.py, base.py, cut_caa.py, audio_feats.py, emo_run.py, jev_gapfill.py, assemble.py.
- External (caa) consults:
  - cut_caa.py cut 19 consults from the room tape mirror; 8 more had low coverage.
  - caa consults have no Nemotron output, so the Sarvam STT segments and per-segment caa roles stand in.
- Per-consult features:
  - language: duration-weighted Sarvam segment language
  - English share
  - doctor and patient talk-time share, turns per minute and interruptions: Nemotron diarization, with roles from llm-roles, then text-judge, then none
  - time of the first surgery mention
  - patient valence and arousal, and their post-minus-pre change around the recommendation (at least 5 s of patient speech on each side)
  - Jev pitch items f02, f03, f05, f06, f07, f18
  - specialty, doctor prior, age, sex, payer, doctor gender
- ale.py unions the expand anchors with the ft-audio non-anchors, sets the labels and seals the rows.
- model2.py (machinery in model.py):
  - random forest; repeated stratified group 5-fold x 10 repeats, grouped by patient
  - AUC of repeat-averaged OOF scores; patient-bootstrap 95% CI
  - permutation importance: 10 repeats x 5 permutations
  - feature sets: audio_f07, audio_nof07, pulse_only, audio8_old
- desc.py: converters vs non-converters. Mann-Whitney test and Cliff's delta for continuous features; Fisher test and odds ratio for Jev items at p >= 0.5.

**Engines and model ids**
- Lane LLM: Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock.
  - ft-audio: global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5 (DONE.md).
  - ft-cohortb-expand: "Opus 5.5 on Bedrock"; the model id is not stated in its DONE.md.
- STT: Sarvam saaras-v3. The outputs were already in the REB track store for all 125 v1 consults (COVERAGE.md).
- Diarization: Nemotron-3-Diarization, also pre-stored in the REB store.
- Emotion:
  - Odyssey `odyssey_wavlm_dim.v1` = HF `3loi/SER-Odyssey-Baseline-WavLM-Multi-Attributes`, offline cached weights, GPU on the lab box.
  - The revision is not written to lane outputs (emo_run.py drops the extractor's `rev`). The HF cache on the lab box has refs/main = snapshot 00d0e12ba9bf.
  - ft-audio and expand ran it on 19 Oct (expand: 50 consults, 1,230 windows).
  - emotion2vec and Clef were not run.
- Jev v2: 31 questions plus preamble (p2run_v2), API model string "jev-latest" (jevpitch.py).
  - The served model name is recorded per row in the jev jsonl files and was not opened here.
  - Gap-fill runs: ft-audio 25 consults; expand 41 consults (49 attempted, 8 failed for lack of usable English text).
- RF775 membership: "Haiku-confirmed" (convert-lead). The Haiku model id is not stated in the ft-audio files.

**Checks/tests run**
- seal_check.py traces every id in out/ft-audio through split_guard: 0 H.
- Surgery yes/no agrees 49/49 between labels v2 and ft4a on the overlapping consults.
- Expand overlap: the 49 shared consults match ft-audio exactly on the audio features.
- f07 leakage check: hand-coded categories A-E. 4 of 7 early positives had a date fixed in the room.
- Refute v1 (PASS-WITH-FIXES): fixes 1-5 were applied in v2.
- Refute v2 (PASS-WITH-FIXES, I-1 to I-5): cohort, labels and descriptives reproduce. Ale strict credit gives 138/23. Only f06 survives BH correction. The specialty importance CI > 0 does not reproduce. The doctor-adjusted f06 p is 0.12.

**Key files**

  - `code/ale.py` 6bdbd99902ba; `model2.py` 2192f6dff1d2; `model.py` 6e54bd023979; `desc.py` 567607d7cf87

- `~/oc/forest-lead/ft/out/ft-cohortb-expand/DONE.md` ce958b63bd53; `COVERAGE.md` 2eae75e18e62; `LEAKAGE_f07.md` 9722cc4b4abc; `features_all.parquet` a3fc972290ee

### M-FTC. Counselling-call text (lane ft-calltext v2; refute ft-refute-calltext) [S10, S11, S17]
**Collection**
- Funnel T1 pulls, which forest-lead confirmed as canonical:
  - `t1a-call-ids-db16.sql` (db 16): prescription_uid -> rh_surgery_leads -> contact_hash (join key only) -> rh_runo_call_log. Every call on a lead's contact since 2025-12-01, on or after the lead day. Output `~/oc/forest-lead/funnel/in/t1a-call-ids.tsv`, written 19 Oct 16:45: 23,024 rows, 20,047 calls.
  - `t1b01..t1b11-call-summaries.sql` (db 13): Runo call summary text for the listed call_ids, calls in [Rx, Rx+90]. 6,098 calls; the latest summary_ts per call is used.
- The SQL headers say "forest-lead funnel T1". Who executed the pulls is not stated in the SQL files.
- No Bolna pull exists.
- The lane ran the join, tagging and model on 19 Oct (DONE 17:50; v2 fixes about 19:45). The refute ran 17:42-17:57, and its v2 delta check 19:29-19:33.

**Cut-off and leakage rules**
- D split only (RD-0 lock RD0-2026-10-18-52959278edd8). Labels v2 (0aefc00f425b): h14 final at >= 18 d, h90 at >= 94 d. Ale added in v2.
- Only Rx >= 2026-01-01 (Dec 2025 has no leads).
- Window calls: days 0-14 after the Rx and strictly before the surgery day. Dates are day-level, so a call on the surgery day is dropped.
- Calls tagged outcome_revealed are always dropped (483 verified, 1 claimed).
- Primary model: post-Rx landmark at T = 3 and 7.
  - Only calls with day_from_rx < T are used.
  - Episodes with surgery <= T are dropped.
  - Label = surgery in (T, H].
  - Call features never feed an at-Rx or at-consult model.
- Ambiguity:
  - Cohort P drops the hash-only F1 flag (8 D episodes).
  - Cohort S also drops the 10e2 shared-phone set (516 D).
- The as-is window (ending at surgery) is reported only as a leak demonstration.
- Disclosed residual: Haiku saw chunks of up to 10 calls spanning days 0-14, so context from later calls could shape an earlier call's tag.

**Processing steps**
- ftc_join.py links D episodes to calls on every flagged Rx of the RD-0 cluster (10.tsv plus clusters-2026-10-18). Result: 5,788 episode x call rows, 787 episodes; 3,305 connected; 2,295 with a summary, in 552 episodes.
- ftc_tag.py (Haiku):
  - one request per episode chunk of <= 10 calls; one tag row per call
  - tags: 8 objections, intent, next_step, price_quoted, outcome_revealed
  - each tag's quote must be a whitespace-normalised substring of that call's text (case-insensitive fallback, >= 6 chars), otherwise the tag is dropped
  - 5M-token pre-flight cap
- ftc_model.py (v2 state):
  - RF using the FT3 machinery: 400 trees, min_samples_leaf 5, out-of-fold smoothed doctor prior; baseline = features_D.parquet
  - FT3 primary baseline with same-day payer OFF (v2)
  - 5x5 StratifiedGroupKFold on the RD-0 cluster, identical folds for base and base+call
  - pooled OOF AUC, 1,000-draw cluster bootstrap, paired ΔAUC
  - P(Δ <= 0) from the bootstrap and from the repeats
- ftc_table.py turns the result JSONs into V2-TABLES.md.
- ftc_desc.py builds DESC.md:
  - per episode: any verified tag over its window calls with a summary, revealing calls dropped, cohort P
  - converted vs not, Wilson 95% CIs, overall and per specialty, plus the tag audit

**Engines and model ids**
- Lane LLM: Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock, global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5.

### M-FTR. Do calls predict conversion: call text + call audio (lane ft-calltext FTR final v2; refute ft-refute-ftr) [S12, S13]
**Collection**
- No new pulls. Inputs:
  - FTC calls_D, episodes_D and tags_D
  - labels v2 (0aefc00f425b)
  - features_D.parquet 8ec830bfc4d6 and features_D.cols.json 35d252d4490e
  - F1 hash-only f1-2026-10-19.tsv e16e146b41c9
  - the ft-rl-feat final call_audio_feats.parquet acca10d995c4, snapshotted to `~/wt/ft-calltext/snap/call_audio_feats_FINAL_10200251.parquet`
- Lane: ft-calltext.
  - watch_ftr.sh reran run_ftr.sh on each stable snapshot (19 Oct 20:37 to 20 Oct 02:06).
  - Final v1 ran 03:22; final v2 (with fixes) ran 04:09-04:13, 20 Oct.
- Refute ft-refute-ftr: own numbers 03:33-03:56; v2 check 04:31-04:35, 20 Oct.

**Cut-off and leakage rules**
- Same as FTC v2: D only, cohort P, Rx >= 2026-01-01, landmark T = 3 and 7, calls with day_from_rx < T, revealing calls dropped.
- F1 at-risk rule (from the rl-feat refute): drop episodes with surgery <= T. The code asserts 0 kept episodes with an event <= T.
- Audio: the ft-rl-feat `_t3`/`_t7` columns only (`_t14` is not a valid landmark for h14 or Ale).
- Fix 1 (leak): when n_calls_tT == 0, every audio feature is set to missing (-1) and aud_has = (n_calls_tT > 0). This is in ftc_model.py rlfeat_to_aud.

**Processing steps**
- run_ftr.sh runs ftc_model.py for h14, h90 and Ale at lm3 and lm7. Models: base, text, text_audio, audio, text_only.
- RF 400 / leaf 5; 5x5 StratifiedGroupKFold on the RD-0 cluster; one split per repeat shared by all models.
- Paired ΔAUC with a 1,000-draw cluster-bootstrap percentile CI on repeat-averaged OOF scores.
- Per-call audio rows are aggregated per landmark: means for shares and emotion, sums for counts, max for tags.
- OOF arrays are saved in model_final2_*.json (fix 3).
- ftc_table.py builds FTR-TABLES-final-v2.md, including Holm step-down over the 18-delta family (text, text_audio and audio vs base x 6) and the 24-delta family. Two-sided bootstrap p, floor 1/1000.
- No-role sensitivity: model_final2_nopat_*.

**Engines and model ids**
- Lane LLM: Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock (FTR-REPORT.md, FTR-TABLES header). The model id global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5 is in DONE.md.
- No new vendor calls in FTR. The audio features come from Sarvam saaras-v3, Odyssey v1 and Haiku 5.5, via ft-rl-feat (see M-RLF).
- Refuter: Claude Opus 5.5 (global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5). No Bedrock, Haiku or Sarvam calls.

**Checks/tests run**
- Lane:
  - F1 assert.
  - aud_has counts equal the refuter's audio-covered at-risk cells: h14 197/19 and 235/11; h90 96/21 and 110/17; Ale 209/23 and 244/11.
  - Holm-adjusted minimum p: 0.972 (18 deltas), 1.000 (24 deltas).
- Refute:
  - Counts, F1 and folds pass. It found the file-membership leak and ran a masked rerun. The h90 T7 "audio CI excludes 0" does not reproduce.
  - It used its own baseline pipeline (fold seeds 0 and 50) and re-bootstrapped the lane's stored OOF (4,000 draws).
  - v2 PASS: point estimates exact; the CI bounds move about 0.003 with the bootstrap seed.
  - Its re-bootstrap gives Ale T7 text_audio-base [+0.003, +0.158] where the lane reports a lower bound of -0.000. Nothing survives Holm.

**Collection**
- RL-1 index of the Runo recording store (run rl1-20261019; the rerun at 8c6a38ab): 800,244 objects, 237.5 GB, months 2025-10 to 2026-10. Index at `~/eta-data/forest/rl/index/rl1-20261019.jsonl.gz`.
- W1 pulls (db 13, read-only, no raw phone; DONE.md says gating-lead ran them):
  - `rl-w1a-md5-cohort.sql`: md5(last10 of phone) -> hashed patient id, n_uhid. 2,473 rows.
  - `rl-w1b-md5-calls.sql`: Runo calls on the phones of D flagged-Rx patients since 1 Nov 2025. 15,240 calls, 0 phone conflicts.
  - `rl-w1c-rx-uhid.sql`: 1,233 Rx -> hashed patient id. Output written 19 Oct 18:46.
  - The md5 files were re-keyed and moved to _to_delete.
- RL-2 cohort: 1,233 D episodes / 1,231 patients.
  - matched: 3,285 files = 3,244 calls / 563 patients
  - ambiguous: 4,479 (excluded, never guessed)
  - outside window: 352
- RL-3 fetch: 1,407 calls / 414 episodes / 0.53 GB, to `~/eta-data/forest/rl/audio/` (0700 dir, 0600 files).
- Lane ft-rl-run worked on 19 Oct, 18:30 to 20:26 (DONE).
- STT:
  - Wave of 933 calls in 3 shards of 311.
  - Runs: lane b 19 Oct 20:26-20:32 (paused); lane a 20 Oct 00:08-00:25 (killed in the wave-wide pause); lane c 19 Oct 23:58-20 Oct 00:10 (breaker on 429s).
  - Final runs on 20 Oct: c 01:27-01:45; a 01:50-02:01; b 02:27-02:35.
  - Transcripts: `~/eta-data/forest/rl/stt/<key_id>.json` (0600).

**Cut-off and leakage rules**
- Preregistered rules (RL1-REPORT):
  - window = call date (IST) in [Rx-30d, Rx+90d]
  - multi-Rx tiebreak: a call goes to the latest Rx on or before it
  - a call on the decision date counts as post_decision
  - a phone mapping to anything other than exactly 1 patient record is AMBIGUOUS and excluded
- Fetch set (ftrl_select.py):
  - days 0-14 after the Rx, pre-decision, before the surgery event
  - calls that ft-calltext Haiku tagged outcome_revealed are dropped (193)
  - excluded: 456 before the Rx, 608 after day 14, 598 post_decision
- Shard set (ftrl_manifest.py, spend guard G1-G7):
  - D episodes only; days 0-13; day 0 only for calls after the consult time
  - ffprobe >= 20 s; dropped 223 short or unprobeable, 12 on day 14, 227 on day 0 before the consult
- Final set: 933 calls / 369 episodes / 2,298.3 min.
  - Forest-lead rulings removed 9 day-0 rows with an unverified time and 3 G2-violation rows (call_ts = upload time) from the first 945.
  - 6 flagged rows remain, with ts_is_call_day = 0, on days 1-13.
- rl_stt.py gates every run before any network call:
  - eta-lab-run pin c41bb34a; manifest membership
  - split_guard D-only (list pin 52959278edd8)
  - G1 D episode; G2 window; G3 ffprobe; G4 skip-existing and retry once; G5 GO file with N/minute caps and a 1.05 hard stop; G6 no off-shard calls; G7 ledger
  - one ledger line per job in `~/eta-data/sarvam-ledger/forest-runo/`

**Processing steps**

### M-RLF. Call-audio features (lane ft-rl-feat; refute ft-refute-rlfeat) [S14]
**Collection**
- Inputs:
  - rl/manifest.tsv; only the in_shard a/b/c rows are used (474 fetched but never sharded rows are excluded)
  - the 933 STT JSONs
  - labels v2
  - the ft-calltext tagger code
- Lane runs:
  - code built on synthetic fixtures, 19 Oct 18:30-21:32
  - partial runs: 19 Oct about 20:37 (51 calls); 20 Oct 00:45 (505); 01:40 (573)
  - FINAL run 20 Oct 02:51 (933/933)
- Refute: 20 Oct 03:06-03:12.

**Cut-off and leakage rules**
- D only: manifest episode_ids not in labels split D are dropped before any STT or audio is read, and the output asserts split == D.
- Windows: day_from_rx < T for T = 3, 7 and 14 (`_t3`, `_t7`, `_t14`). Calls on or after event_date are dropped (0 such calls). Calls with a verified outcome_revealed tag are dropped (140).
- 11 calls that failed tagging twice are excluded, because they cannot be screened.
- Refute F1: the file still holds episodes whose surgery came before T, so downstream models must keep only the at-risk set (days_to_surgery >= T). FTR applies this.
- Roles: the STT has no counsellor/patient labels, so roles come from a phrase lexicon, falling back to "more words = counsellor". The fallback was used for 678/933 calls (73%), which makes the patient-side features noisy.

**Processing steps**
- run_all.sh runs four steps:
  1. rlf_prep.py: talk time, turns, interruptions, roles, speaker-labelled text and Odyssey windows.
  2. rlf_emo.py: Odyssey valence, arousal and dominance per window, on the lab-box GPU, with a RAM guard.
  3. rlf_tag.py: the ftc_tag.py schema, prompt and quote verifier on the transcripts. Multilingual quotes are kept in their original script. 3M-token cap.
  4. rlf_assemble.py: per-episode features and COVERAGE.md.
- Work files: `~/eta-data/forest/rl/feat/` (0700).
- Output: 369 D episodes x 168 columns. Episodes with a call in the window: T3 255, T7 306, T14 344.

**Engines and model ids**
- Lane LLM: Claude Sonnet 5.5 on Bedrock, global.anthropic.claude-sonnet-5-5 (DONE.md).
- Tagger: Claude Haiku 5.5, `global.anthropic.claude-haiku-5-5`, loaded from ~/wt/ft-calltext/code/ftc_tag.py MODEL. About 2.1M input tokens (cap 3M).
- Emotion: Odyssey `odyssey_wavlm_dim.v1` (HF 3loi/SER-Odyssey-Baseline-WavLM-Multi-Attributes), timbre-nightly venv, cached weights.
  - Revision not recorded in lane outputs. The lab-box HF cache refs/main = 00d0e12ba9bf.
  - 9,823 windows scored, 0 errors.
- STT input: Sarvam saaras-v3 (M-RL).
- Refuter: Claude Opus 5.5 (global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5). No vendor calls.

**Checks/tests run**
- Lane: synthetic tests with 6 consult recordings relabelled as fake calls (235 windows, 0 errors; 3 tagged calls, 0 unverified), plus an undiarised-shape fixture.
- Refute (PASS-WITH-FIXES):
  - split: 0 H anywhere
  - window counts reproduce exactly for all three T values
  - F1 at-risk issue found: T7 h14 is 17/245 once restricted
  - F2: 247 calls were re-tagged; the last tag line is used
  - quotes exact up to case: 2,368/2,374
  - talk-time features on 50 random calls match the per-call table
  - roles: the fallback agrees with the lexicon on 87% of lexicon calls
  - no transcript text and no identifiers in the parquet or markdown

**Key files**
- S14: `~/oc/forest-lead/ft/out/ft-rl-feat/call_audio_feats.parquet` acca10d995c4; `DONE.md` c50c3e4b8c14; `COVERAGE.md` f33f82368e3f
- Code (out/code and ~/wt/ft-rl-feat/code are identical): run_all.sh b28d8bf2b42b; rlf_prep.py 37588889a3c2; rlf_emo.py 9003469b4e32; rlf_tag.py 8401d0db9be4; rlf_assemble.py 3858f7392d2f; mk_synth.py ddfde059ea8b; mk_synth_nolabel.py 1a9c50366a95

## METHODS, group 3: FT6 translation, ft-text2 (Bolna + Jev), ft-engines (voice engines)
Read 20 Oct 2026 (IST), read-only. sha = sha256[:12]. "The lab box" = the lab box. No ids, quotes or native text are copied here.

### M-FT6. Translation check (palimpsest; FT7 section 7; tags S15, S20)
- **Collection**
  - System: Palimpsest REB store on the lab box (read-only). Native-language Sarvam STT turns plus today's served English (translate track, else gapfill track, as consult_bundle serves it).
  - Candidate list: ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/ft6-candidates.txt (83 uids; sha dc98ac0c3e95; written 19 Oct 16:33). The picker took 20 (te 2 = all, hi 8, kn 10; seed 6, --pick 20; 36 skipped as en/bn, 23 not in any source) per ~/oc/palimpsest/reports/FT6-REPORT.md.
  - Consult date range of the 20: not stated in DONE.md. The pipeline description (Saaras for consult clips from 8 Oct; Mayura for September CAA consults) is from code, DONE.md section 1.
  - Pulled by: palimpsest lane (palimpsest-architect orders; builder "Sonnet 5.5" per FT6-REPORT.md line 1). run1 per-consult files written 19 Oct 19:33-23:45 IST; run1.log 23:46.
  - run2 (FT6b, adds a Saaras v4 arm "V4") ran 20 Oct 07:09-07:29 IST. It is NOT cited in FT7 section 7 (S15/S20 are run1).
- **Cut-off and leakage**
  - D split only. The tool reads the sha-pinned heldout guard (fail-closed) and skips held windows (ft6_translate_compare.py consult_turns / window_held).
  - Refuter mapped all 20 consults via patient_key -> cluster -> split_guard (lock RD0-2026-10-18-52959278edd8, pin checked): D 20, H 0 [S20 item 5].
  - Not a predictive lane: no outcome labels used, so no landmark cut-off applies.
- **Processing**
  - Per native STT turn (4,407 turns), three versions: CUR = today's English (93% Saaras speech-translate, 7.4% = 326 turns Mayura gapfill); S = Sarvam Mayura on the native text; M = Gemini on the native text. G (Google Translate v3) skipped: no credential (summary.json arms_skipped).
  - Turn selection: deterministic weighted lexicon on the NATIVE text only (refusal, fear, insult, cost, kinship, imperative), top 8 per consult = 160 judged turns, min 8 chars. Refuter: 82% of judged turns carry a negation/refusal term vs 13% of all turns.
  - Judge: blind labels A-C, shuffle seeded by sha256(seed|uid|turn); scores 0-2 per version for preserved valence and intent; any reversal ("flip") must quote native and English text; a quote that is not a whitespace-normalised substring is dropped (1 dropped in run1, raw reply not stored, so unauditable).
  - Judge isolation: cc-bedrock.sh print mode with --no-session-persistence --bare --tools "", cwd = --out (fix round 1).
  - Budget: USD 15 hard stop checked before every send; spent USD 2.43 (S 0.41, M 1.93, judge 0.09). run2: USD 1.88 of a 10 cap.
- **Engines and model ids (as recorded)**
  - Saaras (CUR source): saaras:v3 (kit/reb/sarvam_lane.py BASE_PARAMS model "saaras:v3", engine sarvam-saaras-v3).
  - Mayura: mayura:v1, target en-IN (kit/reb/translate_gapfill.py MODEL), used for both arm S and the CUR gapfill.
  - Gemini: google/gemini-3.8-flash via OpenRouter, temperature 0 (GEMINI_MODEL). Not Google Translate.
  - Judge: global.anthropic.claude-haiku-5-5 on Bedrock via ~/oc/bedrock/cc-bedrock.sh (summary.json judge_engine "bedrock-cli:global.anthropic.claude-haiku-5-5"). Jev judge fallback removed in fix round 2.
  - run2 only: Sarvam saaras:v4 speech-translate on consult.flac, one batch job per consult (run2/summary.json arm_labels).
  - Lane agent serving model: not stated in DONE.md; FT6-REPORT.md says "Builder, Sonnet 5.5"; commits c4891121 and 4a2ad5e8 are co-authored "Claude Sonnet 5.5".
  - Refuters: code refutes (FT6-VERDICT, FT6-VERDICT-2) "Opus refuter", model id not stated in those files; results refute (S20) states "Opus 5.5, global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5".
- **Checks and tests**
  - Code refute 1 (~/oc/palimpsest/reports/FT6-VERDICT.md, head 23f0a8e6): FAIL on items 7 (budget stop) and 8 (judge transcript/hooks); suite 827 passed.
  - Code refute 2 (FT6-VERDICT-2.md, head 01309686): FAIL on 7 (Jev fallback unbudgeted) only; suite 805/832 passed depending on venv.

All counts reproduce (20 consults, 4,407 turns, 160 judged, means 0.950/1.281/1.725, flips 20/9/0, wins and ties by language); kept quotes 29/29 verbatim; blinding checked (all 6 label orders, no position bias, p~0.14); bootstrap over 20 consults, 10k resamples. ### M-TEXT2. Bolna transcripts + Jev cross-check (lane ft-text2; FT7 sections 4b and 4e layer 3; tag S25)
- **Collection**
  - Bolna AI-call transcripts: Metabase database 13, read-only, pulled by gating-lead on 20 Oct with ~/oc/forest-lead/sql/ft8-bolna.sql (patients of the 1,233 D-split anchor Rx; phone link made inside the database, last-10-digit match; no phone leaves it). Output ~/eta-data/forest/ft8/bolna/bolna-d.tsv (sha 49d3780f1f48): 604 rows = 303 calls on 150 D Rx. ft-text2 ran 8 count-only aggregate queries (same 604/150); its own text-pull SQL code/b1-bolna.sql.tpl was NOT run.
  - Runo call summaries (S1, 2,185 calls): ft-calltext tags_D.jsonl (d86ad5d43700). Runo call-audio transcripts (S2, 922 calls, 1,197 rows, last row per call): ft-rl-feat tags.jsonl (0a3f5b87abb3).
  - Date range: landmark cells use Rx >= 1 Jan 2026 (COVERAGE.md); calendar end date not stated in DONE.md/COVERAGE.md.
  - Lane: ft-text2, launched by newcat-orch; PLAN pre-registered 20 Oct 04:35 IST; DONE ~04:58 IST; refute 05:36; fixes ~05:45.
- **Cut-off and leakage**
  - D split only: labels.parquet and features_D filtered to split == D on load, asserted; refuter A8: 0 non-D episode ids in any output.
  - Window as ft-calltext: IST calendar day, 0 <= day_from_rx <= 14, call day < surgery day (2 rows dropped), 37 duplicate rows removed -> 37 calls on 29 episodes. Landmark T applied downstream (day_from_rx < T); at-risk = surgery > T.
  - Time: rows with offset converted to IST; the Jan 2026 no-offset rows read as UTC (inferred from the hour histogram, not documented). Refuter: same result if read as IST.
  - Leak screen: outcome_revealed is never removed by Jev; Jev p >= 0.8 adds 12 calls (S1 6, S2 6) to Haiku's 628.
  - Cohort P keeps phone-ambiguous episodes (hash-only rule); cohort S drops all ambiguity-flagged ones (34/37 calls on a shared phone).
- **Processing**
  - Bolna tagging with the ft-calltext tagger ftc_tag.py (sha 54d97b97d0f6, schema/verifier/model unchanged) plus one sentence that the caller is an automated voice agent; every positive needs a code-verified exact quote.
  - Jev: 16 binary tags per call, one request per call with 16 "noul" questions; positive at p >= 0.5; contradiction = Haiku positive with p < 0.2 (or Haiku negative with p >= 0.8).
  - Model reference labels (not human): Opus, blind to Haiku/Jev, same question wording as Jev. Sample A seed 20 = 80 S1 + 60 S2 + all 37 S3 (177). Arbitration sample C seed 21, up to 8 contradictions per tag per direction (195 calls, 206 draws; 5 unverified quotes excluded).
  - Pre-registered tag verdict rules (DROP / FLAG / KEEP) in PLAN.md; call-level Jev filter = Haiku AND NOT jev_contradicts.
  - Language: Bolna text is English in Latin script (code/lang_stats.py -> lang_stats.txt); no Saaras or translation.
  - Rebuild order (DONE.md): bolna_join -> bolna_tag -> make_sample A -> jev_run sample/all -> ref_label A -> make_sample C -> ref_label C -> analyze final -> dropin -> bolna_episode.
- **Engines and model ids (as recorded)**
  - Tagger: Haiku 5.5, global.anthropic.claude-haiku-5-5 (30 requests, 37.6k in / 10.8k out).
  - Cross-check: Jev jev-1.13.0 (requested as jev-latest, via ~/oc/diar-lab-wt/kit/reb/jev_client.py); 3,144/3,144 calls, 0 errors; ~3.87M in / 1.00M out.
  - Reference labeller: Opus 5.5, global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5 on Bedrock, steward ZDR route (ref_label.py MODEL); 372 requests, 710k / 279k.
  - Lane agent serving model: "Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock (global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5)" (DONE.md line 2; ft-text2.prompt). Refuter: Opus 5.5 (REPORT line 1; exact id not stated in the report).
- **Checks and tests**
  - Jev calibration on A: AUC 0.94-1.00, ECE 0.044-0.118, Brier 0.006-0.094. Kappa Haiku vs Jev on all calls 0.44-0.90.
  - Verdicts: DROP obj_cost, obj_going_elsewhere (clears the 20% rule by 1 call); FLAG 9; KEEP 5.
  - Refute ft-text2-oref at 6aa91f741083: PASS-WITH-FIXES; tests 22/22 (work/harness.py 18 + work/synth.py 4); mutants 16/16; 12 of 20 allowed Jev reruns (max |dp| 0.07). OWN-NUMBERS written before reading analyze.py; reruns byte- or frame-identical. 5 fixes, all applied (DONE.md Refute section).

### M-ENG. Voice engines on Runo calls and OPD consults (lane ft-engines; FT7 section 4e layers 4-7; tag "-")
- **Collection**
  - Set A: 933 D-split Runo calls (~/eta-data/forest/rl/manifest.tsv, audio + Sarvam STT JSON from the ft-rl-feat pull) -> 369 D episodes; 911 calls have >= 1 speech window.
  - Set B: 211 OPD consults = 125 ft-audio cohort-B D consults + 86 FT4 Ale-cohort extension consults; clips and REB tracks from the REB store.
  - Date range: not stated in DONE.md or COVERAGE.md.
  - Lane: ft-engines, launched 20 Oct by newcat-orch; built 346a62d (claim 05:40 IST); fixes 15b31b3 ~06:30; DONE.md last edit 06:51. Work files ~/eta-data/forest/ft-engines (0700).
- **Cut-off and leakage**
  - Set A eligibility at landmark T = ft-rl-feat rule recomputed from its work files: day_from_rx < T, STT ok, call_day < event_date, tagged (Haiku) and not outcome_revealed; 11 untaggable calls excluded. T in {3, 7, 14}. Every engine feature aggregates only eligible calls of that T. At-risk rule left to the model.
  - Set B: consult audio only (T = 0).
  - Split: D asserted in assemble.py; split_guard (pin 52959278edd8...) on every set B consult; 37 extension consults not in linked.jsonl were guarded via the labels.parquet cluster of their episode, all D. seal_check FILES_WITH_H {} (both refuters).
  - Labels moved to a sidecar file (engine_calls_label_sidecar.parquet), masks only (refute fix).
- **Processing**
  - Roles set A: each STT segment to the Nemotron speaker it overlaps most; rlf_prep lexicon per speaker (lexicon 260, lexicon_weak 200, fallback 470, single 3). Windows take the role of the speaker covering >= 60%. Role-specific columns use only non-fallback-role calls (spec section 4; refute fix); role-free `_all_` columns use all eligible calls. forest-lead ruling 06:31: primary = lexicon + lexicon_weak; *_lex2 twins unused.
  - Roles set B: llm-roles > text-judge > sarvam-doctor-map (caa-labels for external) carried to Nemotron segments; doctor windows 8 s, max 48 per consult.
  - Odyssey: existing rows reused (ft-rl-feat emo.jsonl, 9,823 windows; ft-audio/cohortb-expand), re-roled; new runs only for B doctor windows (4,619 + 1,674).
  - Clef-omni: one request per item x role; role windows concatenated with 0.15 s gaps, thinned to <= 240 s; question set timbre-clef-v1; no outcome-stating question.
  - WavLM: 7 probabilities + raw 1024-d mean embedding per role; no PCA fitted (fit in-fold).
  - Compute: lab box T4 GPU (gpu.lock gate) and a GCP L4 GPU (set B, 16 kHz mono FLAC, D only, same venvs/weights/code). Clef spend US$0.516.

### M-G4.1 ft-bench: benchmarks (a) stated intent, (b) Jev, (c) Claude Opus 5.5; leak audit; calibration and permutation harness [S29, S30]

#### Collection / inputs
- Run by lane ft-bench, launched by newcat-orch (.bc/ft-bench.prompt). Work: 20 Oct 2026, 04:26-07:20 IST (git 8136a8e 04:34 to 44520f9 07:20). The frozen-forest read-out (BENCH-TABLES-frozen-0910.md, CALIB-frozen-0910.md) was run later, 09:10-09:26 IST, by lane ft-forest via its final.sh, which calls ft-bench `code/run_frozen.sh` (final_run.log).
- Episodes, labels (h14, h90, Ale), landmarks (T = 3, 7), clusters and folds: exactly the ft-calltext FTR v2 ones (model_final2_*.json OOF arrays) (PREREG.md).
- Consult text: the episode's recorded anchor consult (ft-cohortb-expand inventory, duplicates dropped), native Sarvam STT, not the Saaras English (fable FT6 ruling). Roles from role.llm-roles / caa-labels when present, else "Speaker N".
- Call text: Runo calls with Sarvam STT (ft-rl-stt, 933 D calls). Speakers = diarisation ids.
- (a) intent sources: ft-calltext tags_D (Runo summaries) + rl-feat tags.jsonl (Runo STT). Consult fallback: Jev v2 f10/f11/f12 (p2v2 jev.jsonl, ft-audio/cohortb gap-fills, rf775_members.jsonl). These were computed on Saaras English; this is flagged in PREREG Addendum 1.
- Design C: 201 consults (196 with text) from ft-audio features_ale + ft-cohortb-expand features_all. Rows = forest-lead designC_cohort (ale_strict 138/23; h14_ge18 114/12).
- Date range of transcripts: not stated in DONE.md/PREREG.md beyond "before T". FTR v2 rows are Rx >= 2026-01-01 (ft-forest forest_build.py header).
- Text and raw LLM outputs are kept in ~/eta-data/forest/bench/ (0700). They were not opened.

#### Cut-off and leakage rules
- A call is kept only if: day_from_rx < T; call_date < event date; it is Haiku-tagged (untagged calls are dropped because they cannot be screened); and it is not outcome_revealed (verified OR unverified). After fix 7, ANY of its tag records counts. Consult kept only if consult day < T (asserted).
- call_date is authoritative (upstream ft-rl-run ruling). Sensitivity: drop the rows that use the 4 ts_is_call_day=0 calls (fix 8).
- Variants (Addendum 4-5, POST-HOC):
  - lag1: calls on day >= T-1 removed.
  - consult: consult text only.
  - screen v1: whole calls matching the pre-auth/admission/OT-preop/payment regex are dropped, plus matching consult turns.
  - screen2 = v1 + an insurance/policy/TPA/mediclaim/health card/Ayushman category, in Latin and native script (fix 2). Calls are renumbered after the screen (fix 1/3).
  - Layer-8 PRIMARY (spec v3.3) = opus_screen2_p14/p90. The unscreened opus_* score is a proxy-inflated sensitivity.
- Outcome-proxy rule: f07 analogy (date/slot fixed, admission/pre-op, pre-auth filed). Opus category audit `bench_audit.py`: flags only, no text out, all 551 covered rows (AUDIT_ALL=1).
- D split only; H never read (stated in every lane file).

#### Processing
- (a) Stated intent: the last stated intent before T, coded declines=0, undecided=1, wants_surgery=2, none=-1.
  - Pre-registered as a one-feature RF (400 trees, leaf 5, FTR 5x5 folds). This was declared INVALID post-hoc (Addendum 3: null OOF AUC 0.32-0.46, mean 0.40 on 4 shuffles). The raw ordinal code (no training) is the Holm benchmark.
- (b) Jev: P(yes) of type 'noul' on two questions, p14 and p90. h14 and Ale use p14; h90 uses p90. 2 of 551 requests returned http 400 (the 49k and 63k character inputs); they were not retried and count as missing. DONE.md later counts 4 http 400s in total.
- (c) Opus: one request per (episode, T), not repeated. Returns p14, p90 and booked.
- Prompts pre-registered 04:34 IST, before any score; prompt JSON sha 865e5843df5e (PREREG.md; equals bench_llm.PSHA per ft-refute-bench A6).
- Scoring is "as given" (no training). Populations:
  - ALL at-risk: rows without a score get the median of the covered scores (Addendum 1).
  - COVERED: rows with text or a stated intent.
- AUC with a 1,000-draw cluster bootstrap (RD-0 cluster_key, seed 20261019); the boot / boot_delta code is copied from ftc_model.
  - Paired Δ (forest − benchmark) on identical rows.
  - FTR clusters are singletons, so the bootstrap is effectively iid (LEAK-AUDIT).
- Holm:
  - BENCH-TABLES-frozen-0910.md: family of 18 (FULL − benchmark, ALL population, 3 labels x 2 T x 3 benchmarks).
  - Two-sided bootstrap p = 2·min(P(Δ≤0), P(Δ≥0)), floor 1/1000.
  - The 24-test F-bench family (18 B + 6 C) is computed in ft-forest BENCH-C-FOREST.md (see M-G4.2).
- Frozen read-out: `run_frozen.sh "$O/model_frozen_*.json" FULL,L7` with FOREST_MODELS=FULL:text_audio,L7:base. So the "text_audio" column = FULL and "base" = L7 (ft-forest DONE.md).
  - **Caveat:** the header lines of BENCH-TABLES-frozen-0910.md still say "Forest = FTR v2 base and text_audio ... (stand-in)". That text is stale.
- Calibration harness `calib.py`:
  - Brier, no-skill Brier and Brier skill.
  - Reliability: 10 quantile bins with Wilson CIs.
  - Slope and intercept: logistic regression of y on logit(p). Plus CITL and ECE.
  - 1,000 cluster-bootstrap CIs, seed 20261019.
  - Two recalibrations:
    - nested_platt (spec version): inner GroupKFold(3) on the training folds; needs a refit.
    - crossfit_platt (approximation): the map is fitted on the other folds' OOF.
  - Folds are regenerated as StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle, RS+rep), RS 20261019.
- Permutation ranking (calib.perm_rank): grouped, refit per fold, 10 permutations, 2.5-97.5 percentiles.

#### Engines and model ids
- (c) and the leak audit: Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock, `global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5`. Default effort, no temperature control, maxTokens 4000. Input-token cap raised 5M → 10M → 16M (bench_llm.py:14).
  - Spend: 18.55M input / 0.63M output tokens (DONE.md).
- (b) Jev: "jev-latest, typesafe systemone, 'noul' P(yes)" (PREREG.md), called via `jevpitch.jev_call` (timeout 180 s). Each response's `model` field is stored in the score files, which were not opened. Jev version/revision: not stated in PREREG.md/DONE.md. Cost $0.
- Upstream engines in the inputs: Sarvam STT (native), Haiku tags (outcome_revealed flags), Jev f10-f12 (consult fallback). Versions are not stated in ft-bench files.
- Serving LLM of the lane agent: Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock (DONE.md line 1; .bc/ft-bench.prompt; BUILDER-CLAIM.txt). No model id is given for the serving model.

#### Checks and tests
- test_bench.py 7/7 (invariants: no text dated ≥ T, no outcome-revealing or untagged call, consult day < T).
- test_bench_ext.py (lane ft-bench-tests, fixes 5+6), landed 07:17: 56 passed, 0 failed.
  - The ~/wt/ft-bench-tests copy was updated after landing (R2 item F): 58 passed, 0 xfailed; 23/23 + 4 mutants killed (ft-bench-tests DONE.md).
- calib selftest. The harness reproduces the FTR text_audio OOF AUC exactly and the refit AUC 0.668 (h14 lm7).
- Refute ft-refute-bench (HEAD 21a7f2a), Claude Opus 5.5 Bedrock: PASS-WITH-FIXES. Tests 19/19; mutants 13/16 (2 equivalent). Top issue: "T=7 level" was false for h90.
- Refute ft-refute-bench2:
  - R1 (HEAD 2d8a0c0): PASS-WITH-FIXES. Mutants 12/16. 30/40 Bedrock re-ask requests: r 0.98-0.99, median |Δp14| 0.00.
  - R2 (HEAD cb9940f / eb5bed7): PASS-WITH-FIXES (wording only). Fixes 1-10 verified; layer 8 opus_screen2 cleared. Sandbox rebuilds are byte-identical to every input and layer-8 file.
- Fixes 1-4 and 7-10 were applied in Addendum 6 (POST-HOC, 06:55 IST). Addenda 3-6 are labelled post-hoc.
- **Not refuted by the bench refuters:** the frozen read-out BENCH-TABLES-frozen-0910.md / CALIB-frozen-0910.md (written 09:10-09:26, after both refutes). ft-refute-forest covers it; at 10:20 that refute had no REPORT yet.

#### Discrepancies found against FT7-REPORT
- §4f says "Pre-auth words appear in 31% of converters' transcripts vs 3% of non-converters' (ale, day 3)". LEAK-AUDIT v2 states that figure came from a 29+29-row sample and understated the gap. The v2 all-row figure for ale T3 is pre-auth **31% vs 11%**.
- §4f gives Holm p 0.024 for FULL > Jev at h90 T7. That is the 24-test family (BENCH-C-FOREST.md). BENCH-TABLES-frozen-0910.md shows Holm p 0.018 in its own 18-test family. The two are consistent, but the report should name the family.

### M-G4.2 ft-forest: the frozen pre-registered forest, all designs, sensitivities, calibration read-outs [S24, S26, S27, S30]

#### Collection / inputs
- Run by lane ft-forest, launched by forest-lead (.bc/ft-forest.prompt), on the lab box.
  - DESC-jev.md: 06:24 IST.
  - Dry run on the draft spec: 07:22-08:17 (dry/; FOREST-TABLES-DRY.md d40000dd6553).
  - FINAL on the frozen spec: started 08:23:18; forest done 09:10:28; pipeline end 09:28:50 IST (final_run.log).
  - Sensitivities + nested sigmoid: 09:33-09:54 IST (forest-lead order 09:32).
- Inputs (read-only; shas as logged in DONE.md):
  - features_D 8ec830bfc4d6 (+ cols.json 35d252d4490e); labels v2 0aefc00f425b;
  - episodes_D a06fa6e0747f, calls_D 5d2a1c72a6ec, tags_D d86ad5d43700; F1 hash-only f1-2026-10-19.tsv e16e146b41c9;
  - features_ft8 d674e54010cc;
  - tags_D_jev d507107e9884, rlfeat_tags_jev fa7220dcc743, bolna_episode_feats 68025a99cc6e;
  - rl calls_basic 2812b5a2b83e, rl tags.jsonl 0a3f5b87abb3;
  - engine_feats_calls 41f6928db8f0, engine_feats_consults 53f44b7113f8;
  - bench_layer8 453d83f139b7, bench_layer8_c d315521037a9;
  - designC_cohort 254d16c51a0a, features_ale 3f0c529447e2, features_all a3fc972290ee;
  - for the sensitivities: engine_wavlm_emb_calls a888f29087a6, engine_wavlm_emb_consults f440a5d77412.
- Population (spec s2): flagged-Rx episodes from 1 Dec 2025; D 1,233. Design B rows: Rx >= 2026-01-01, cohort P (hash-only F1 ambiguity dropped).
- S24 DESC-jev: h14, cohort P, 463 episodes / 72 converted. Calls in the window (days 0..14, before the surgery day) with a summary.

#### Cut-off and leakage rules
- Design A: Pulse base without the same-day payer. FT8 is cut at midnight before the Rx day (spec s10 table).
- Design B:
  - At-risk rule F1: surgery on or before day T is dropped.
  - Only calls dated before T; outcome-revealing calls (Haiku verified or unverified) are dropped.
  - Ruling 4: layers 4-8 are set missing on episode/T rows with a Jev-added outcome-revealing call before T (lm3 2/1/2 rows, lm7 2/1/3 for h14/h90/ale).
  - Engine columns are missing when n_calls_tT == 0. Role columns are masked when the roles come only from the fallback (n_calls_roleok_tT == 0). The *_lex2 twins are not used.
  - Layer 7: any Clef field that states the outcome is dropped (f07 rule).
  - Layer 8 = opus_screen2 (process talk screened); the unscreened score appears only as a sensitivity.
- Design C: FT4 time-respecting doc_prior_rate; consult layer 3 DROPPED (ruling 08:20, FT6 Saaras-English compliance).
- Every input is D-only or filtered to D on load (asserted). H never read.

#### Processing
- Learner (spec s3, fixed, no search): RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=400, min_samples_leaf=5, max_features='sqrt', random_state=0).
  - NaN → -1, plus has_ flags. PYTHONHASHSEED=0, fixed column order.
  - In-fold doctor prior rate (m = 10; inner GroupKFold(5) OOF) for A and B.
- Designs (spec s5):
  - A (rx): M0 = L0, M1 = L0+L1, M2 = M1 + consult C4-C8.
  - B (lm3 / lm7 x h14/h90/ale): L0..L8 cumulative in a fixed order, plus FULL and SELECTED.
  - C (consult, ale_strict 138/23 primary, h14_ge18 114/12): C0, C1, C4..C8, FULL, SELECTED.
- Layers (spec s4):
  - 0 Pulse; 1 FT8 (42 ft8_* columns); 2 call activity;
  - 3 Haiku tags, Jev-cross-checked (Jev-contradicted positives set to 0) + Bolna;
  - 4 Nemotron; 5 Odyssey; 6 emotion2vec; 7 Clef-omni; 8 Opus screen2 p14/p90.
- CV:
  - A and B: 5-fold x 5-repeat StratifiedGroupKFold(shuffle, RS + rep), RS 20261019, groups = RD-0 cluster_key (the FTR v2 folds).
  - C: StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle, random_state = rep) x 20 repeats (FT4).
- AUC on the repeat-averaged OOF score, plus PR-AUC and Brier.
  - Paired Δ on the same rows and folds.
  - 1,000-draw cluster bootstrap, percentile 95% CI, seed 20261019.
  - p = two-sided 2·min(P(Δ≤0), P(Δ≥0)), floor 1/1000.
- SELECTED: forward pass that keeps a layer if Δ > 0 and P(Δ≤0) ≤ 0.20. It is optimistic and not a test.
- Grouped permutation importance by layer/engine in FULL (M2 for A): folds of repeats 0-1 x 10 permutations; descriptive only.
- Holm families (spec s9, all pre-declared):
  - F-primary 2; F-Ale-Rx 3; F-calls 54 (48 steps + 6 FULL−L0); F-consults 14 (12 + 2): 73 in FOREST-TABLES.md.
  - F-bench 24 (18 B from bench_results-frozen-0910.json + 6 C) in BENCH-C-FOREST.md.
  - F-H 2 (ft-h1shot). Total 99.
  - A gain is claimed only if Holm p < 0.05 and Δ > 0.
- Calibration:
  - B FULL/L7 and C FULL: CALIB-frozen-0910.md via ft-bench run_frozen.sh (calib.py oof: raw RF + the cross-fitted sigmoid approximation).
  - A M0/M1/M2 and C C7: CALIB-rx-consult.md, made "with the same harness (calib.py oof)" (DONE.md). The exact command is not logged in final_run.log or logs/.
  - The spec's nested in-fold sigmoid was NOT in the final run. It was added in the 09:54 sensitivity pass (forest_sens.py kind=platt: inner GroupKFold(3) on the training folds only, ft-bench calib._lr map; FOREST-TABLES-SENS.md §Calibration).
- Sensitivities (reported, not tested; FULL − L0 only): rawtags (B), rolefree (A, B, C), wavlm (3 in-fold PCA components per role of the 1024-d Aniemore wavlm-emotion-v1 mean-pool), l8raw (unscreened Opus), platt. 50/50 jobs written, 0 failed.
- S24 DESC-jev (ftf_desc.py): the ftc_desc design rerun three ways (raw Haiku; Jev-filtered, Haiku positive set to 0 where Jev p < 0.2; Haiku AND Jev p ≥ 0.5). Shared denominator; Wilson 95% CIs; descriptive, no test.

#### Engines and model ids
- No model or vendor calls in this lane (prompt rule). Engine outputs come from upstream lanes.
- The frozen spec s10 records the engines:
  - Nemotron-3 (kit/reb f667ed73); Odyssey (reused, re-roled); WavLM aniemore.v1 (a08f2a01, sensitivity only); emotion2vec_plus_large.v1 (6c303ba9); Clef-omni (no revision stated).
  - Haiku tags with the Jev cross-check (ft-text2); Opus 5.5 layer 8 (ft-bench, prompt sha 865e5843df5e).
- Serving LLM of the lane agent: Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock, `global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5` (DONE.md line 1, forest_done.py).
- S27 spec author: forest-lead. Signed by newcat-orch 08:50 IST. The serving model of forest-lead is not recorded in FT-MODEL-SPEC-20OCT*.md.

### M-G4.3 ft-h1shot: the single sealed-H score of the frozen at-Rx models [S28]

#### Collection / inputs
- Run by lane ft-h1shot, launched by forest-lead (.bc/ft-h1shot.prompt).
  - Build, tests and dry run: 20 Oct, about 05:32-06:40 IST (DONE v3 06:40).
  - GO-H.txt written by forest-lead at 10:00:22 IST.
  - Single run: claimed 10:00:45, finished ok 10:00:55 IST (H_RUN.done; pid recorded).
- Command: `~/.venv-forest/bin/python -I ~/wt/ft-h1shot/code/h1shot.py --run-H --pull-cut day`.
- Training: all labelled D rows, h14 1,101/189 (age ≥ 18 d) and h90 659/150 (age ≥ 94 d). Target: H h14 361/82, h90 224/64. These match spec s8 (h_counts_vs_spec_s8: match).
- Inputs (shas as recorded in H-RESULT.md):
  - labels.parquet 0aefc00f425b; features_D.parquet 8ec830bfc4d6 (column order only); cols.json 35d252d4490e;
  - ft5a/ft3b/ft3c/ft3d.tsv; 10.tsv; clusters-2026-10-18.tsv; f1-s5-2025-12 … 2026-10 (11 files);
  - D ft8-pull-a-day.tsv 7b39ab45f35b, D ft8-pull-b.tsv fd26e29ec835;
  - H ft8-pull-a.tsv 336da3986ae3, H ft8-pull-b.tsv 3289de0ae086. Not opened here; the sealed dir was not read.
- `ls -l` of ~/oc/forest-lead/ft/out/ft-h1shot/sealed/: one file, H_features.parquet, 0600, 115,449 bytes, 10:00. Not opened.

#### Cut-off and leakage rules
- Layer 0 = FT3 via ft3_features_split.py. It is a copy of ft-labels-base ft3_features.py with only the split filter and output made parameters; the same-day payer is excluded.
- Layer 1 = FT8 via the unchanged ft8_build.py v3, at the day cut:
  - cut_ist_midnight = IST midnight of the anchor Rx day, and every src_max_ts strictly before it;
  - pull-B labs need age_d ≥ 1;
  - `--pull-cut` is required and must equal the D build_stats; timestamp mode is refused;
  - every pull row must be an anchor of the split;
  - ft8_build.assert_cutoff runs on D and H in memory BEFORE the claim (fix 1).
- Doctor prior rate (m 10) and encoder levels are fitted on training rows only; H rows get the training rate.
- Seal guards:
  - It refuses without GO-H.txt, or if H_RUN.done exists.
  - It claims H_RUN.done with O_CREAT|O_EXCL.
  - umask 077 and warnings off before any H read.
  - Exceptions print only the type and file:line.
  - The only outputs are sealed/H_features.parquet (0600, dir 0700), H-RESULT.md (metrics only) and H_RUN.done.
- Split: split_guard pin 52959278edd8 (lock RD0-2026-10-18-52959278edd8). No calls, consults, audio or LLM on H (spec s8).

#### Processing
- M0 = layer 0 (29 columns + doctor_rate, doctor_vol); M1 = M0 + all 42 ft8_*.
- RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=400, min_samples_leaf=5, max_features='sqrt', random_state=0). Trained once and scored once. No CV on H.
- Metrics:
  - AUC; PR-AUC with the base rate; Brier with the no-skill Brier; calibration slope and intercept.
  - Δ AUC (M1 − M0): 1,000-draw cluster bootstrap over the target clusters (h14 359, h90 223 clusters), seed 20261020, percentile 95% CI, plus the 97.5% CI (1.25/98.75 percentiles) and P(Δ≤0).
- Holm F-H (2 tests), reading rule pre-declared in DONE v3 before the run:
  - order the two labels by P(Δ≤0), ties to h14;
  - the first is a gain only if its 97.5% CI is above 0;
  - the second only if the first is a gain and its 95% CI is above 0;
  - otherwise "no gain shown".
- Dry run (--dry): fold 0 of StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle, seed 0, groups = cluster_key) stands in for H (246 episodes; train 987). This is a rehearsal, not an estimate.

#### Engines and model ids
- No LLM or vendor calls in the script (prompt rule). sklearn RandomForestClassifier only. The sklearn version is not stated in DONE.md/H-RESULT.md.
- Serving LLM of the lane agent: Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock, `global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5` (DONE.md line 1).
- Watcher/refuter ft-h1shot-ref: Opus 5.5, `global.anthropic.claude-opus-5-5` (report line 1).

#### Checks and tests
- Lane tests: `36 passed in 30.61s` (run_tests.py; pytest via the ft8-clinical pytest_shim). They cover:
  - FT3 copy frame-equal to features_D on 46 columns, also under hash seeds;
  - ft8_build frame-equal to features_ft8 d674e54010cc;
  - the once-only claim; the GO requirement; pre-claim refusals;
  - the seal path on D;
  - the dry output equal to the shipped file;
  - the pull-cut refusals; the fix-1 cutoff quirks (blank or 1970 src_max_ts refused before the claim);
  - the Holm reading (5 cases); the labels-sha and guard-pin refusals.
- Refute ft-h1shot-ref:
  - First refute on 1d3ebd9c1409: PASS-WITH-FIXES (fixes 1 MUST, 2 SHOULD, 3 DECLARE, 4 WATCH).
  - Fix check 06:42 on 7e4a6a358b43: PASS. Tests 36 passed; own dry reproduction exact; 13 guard cases refuse with no flag; mutants flip.
  - H-pull check 07:06 (SQL text and headers only): PREFLIGHT-READY.
- H run watch 10:00-10:01: CLEAN.
  - One process, the exact GO command; h1shot.py 7e4a6a358b43 at launch.
  - H-RESULT id scan: 0 ids. n/pos match s8. Two files plus the flag.

### G6.1 ft8-blocks (S37; refute S41)
- **Who and when.**
  - Lane ft8-blocks, launched by newcat-orch for V (`~/wt/ft8-blocks/.bc/ft8-blocks.prompt`, 11:07).
  - Tables were written at 11:33 IST. The wording was fixed at 11:46 IST, after the refute.
  - The serving model is not stated in the prompt or in FT8-BLOCKS.md.
- **Question.** Which of the 6 FT8 history blocks (FT-MODEL-SPEC-20OCT s10: utilisation 5, comorbidity 10, admissions 1, vitals 4, labs 20, medicines 2, 42 columns in all) produced the design-A M1-M0 AUC loss.
- **Upstream inputs.**
  - ft-forest code imported unchanged from `~/wt/ft-forest/code`: forest_build 18bc1a321db4, forest_lib f1efb3f87172, forest_run e7e5f7ce4566.
  - `ft8-clinical/features_ft8.parquet` d674e54010cc.
  - The frozen `ft-forest/model_frozen_rx_{h14,h90,ale}.json`, used only for the parity check.
- **Split, cut-off and leakage.**
  - D split only, post hoc and descriptive.
  - H (ft8/H, ft-h1shot/sealed) was never read, per FT8-BLOCKS.md.
  - The FT8 day cut and the design-A rows are inherited from forest_build.build('rx', include_l8=False).
  - Labels: h14 n 1101 (189 converters), h90 n 659 (150), Ale n 1144 (213).
- **Processing.**
  - Models:
    - M0 = L0.
    - M1 = L0 + 42 ft8 columns.
    - (a) M0 plus one block.
    - (b) M1 minus one block.
    - (c) M0 plus the 9 has_ flags, plus one any-history flag, or plus has_prior_rx alone.
    - (d) M0 plus the 42 ft8 columns, rows permuted jointly with numpy default_rng(20261019), one seed. (d) was added after (a) to (c) had been seen.
  - Learner: RF(400, min_samples_leaf 5, sqrt, random_state 0), in-fold doctor rate m=10, NaN coded as -1 plus has_ flags.
  - Folds: 5x5 StratifiedGroupKFold (seed 20261019 + rep) on the patient cluster.
  - AUC on the repeat-averaged OOF scores. Paired delta from a 1,000-draw cluster bootstrap (seed 20261019, the same draws for every pair), with a percentile CI and P(Δ<=0).
  - 54 comparisons (18 per label), no multiplicity correction.
  - Run: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 NJ=4 ~/.venv-forest/bin/python -I code/ft8_blocks.py <lab> --out .`, then `ft8_blocks_tables.py DIR`. The summary was written by hand.
- **Engines and model ids.** No LLM or vendor call; scikit-learn RF only, run in ~/.venv-forest.
- **Checks.**
  - M0 and M1 OOF equal the frozen model_frozen_rx_* exactly (max|diff| 0, all 6).
  - An assert checks that the 42 columns equal the union of the blocks.
  - The v1/ run without (d) gives identical numbers for all 16 shared comparisons.
  - `sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS`: 0 failures (checked now).
  - Refute ft8-blocks-ref (Opus, started 11:34, REFUTE.md 11:45): PASS-WITH-FIXES, with 5 wording fixes and no number changed.
  - The refuter's extra shuffle seeds 1 to 4 are in `~/wt/ft8-blocks-ref/scratch/shuf_*.json`.
  - The refuter's scripts (`scratch/chk.py`, `cov.py`, `shuf_seeds.py`) recomputed the tables, coverage and v1 parity, and ran the seed sensitivity.

### G6.2 ft-whoconverts (S38; refute S40)
- **Who and when.**
  - Lane ft-whoconverts for newcat-orch / V (`~/wt/ft-whoconverts/.bc/ft-whoconverts.prompt`, 11:17).
  - Outputs are from 11:32 to 12:09. WHO-CONVERTS.md is stamped 12:10 IST and was revised after the refute.
  - The serving model is not stated in the prompt or the report.
- **Upstream inputs** (shas as listed in WHO-CONVERTS.md Files):
  - features_D 8ec830bfc4d6 (ft-labels-base).
  - labels.parquet 0aefc00f425b (hashed only).
  - features_ft8 d674e54010cc.
  - ft-cohortb-expand features_all a3fc972290ee.
  - ft-audio features_ale 3f0c529447e2.
  - ft-calltext calls_D 5d2a1c72a6ec.
  - pin_pull 7b39ab45f35b.
  - ft-forest model_frozen_rx_h14.json 9ac680310435 and model_frozen_lm7_h14.json ed34c0e93b0f.
  - FOREST-TABLES.md 057ee325677d.
  - ft-forest forest_lib f1efb3f87172 and forest_build 18bc1a321db4.
  - Design B call, engine and layer-8 inputs are loaded through forest_build as frozen.
- **Split, cut-off and leakage.**
  - D split only. Every table read is D-only or filtered to D on load (asserted).
  - forest_build.load_F reads the whole hash-only f1 split TSV (no outcomes) and filters it to D, as disclosed in Limits.
  - Payer: only the payer recorded before the advice is used; the payer recorded at the visit is excluded.
  - Pincode is the value on file at pull time, so it may be leaky; this is disclosed.
  - Day-7 call rows: design B (Rx from 1 Jan 2026, not converted by day 7). Calls that revealed the outcome are dropped, and the refute counted them.
  - Ale is computed on rows at least 14 days old (n 1,129).
- **Processing** (run order `wc_perm.py rx h14`, `wc_perm.py lm7 h14`, `wc_desc.py`, `wc_logit.py rx|lm7 h14` (and TOPK=0), `wc_report.py`; ~/.venv-forest, -I).
  - (1) wc_desc:
    - Rates with Wilson 95% CIs by characteristic.
    - Each level with n >= 10 tested against the rest by two-sided Fisher exact test.
    - Mantel-Haenszel OR within specialty.
    - Doctor spread tested by a 2,000-shuffle permutation test within specialty. Doctors are anonymised by volume, and rates are shrunk to the specialty mean (m 10).
    - BH within each outcome, flag at q < 0.10. Comparisons: h14 100, h90 86, Ale 102.
  - (2) wc_perm: permutation importance per input inside the frozen FULL forests (design A M2, design B day-7 FULL). Refit per training fold, repeats 0 and 1, 3 shuffles per held-out fold.
  - (3) wc_logit: L2 logistic regression (C 1, not tuned) on the Pulse baseline plus the top 10 permutation inputs, with the same folds and in-fold doctor rate. ORs come with 300-draw cluster-bootstrap intervals.
  - (4) History blocks, for all patients and for returning patients only.
- **Engines and model ids.** No LLM or vendor call stated in code headers; scipy, statsmodels and scikit-learn only.
- **Checks.**
  - The refit scores match the frozen OOF to 5e-07 for both designs.
  - Refute ft-refute-whoconverts (independent Opus, VERDICT 12:07 to 12:08): PASS-WITH-FIXES, 0 blockers, 8 fixes and 8 notes, all fixes applied.
    - Own recompute: r1_desc and r5_compare.
    - Confounding: r2, including the gender shuffle p 0.40.
    - Calls with and without outcome-revealing calls: r3.

### G6.3 ft-lexical (S39; refute S42)
- **Who and when.**
  - Lane ft-lexical for newcat-orch / V (prompt 11:17).
  - Run by Claude Opus 5.5 on Bedrock, per DONE.md and PLAN.md. The exact id is not stated for the lane model.
  - PLAN.md was pre-registered at 11:20 or 11:21 IST, before any outcome was joined to text.
  - DONE is stamped 11:55. LEXICAL.md v2 has mtime 12:19. DONE says the fixes were applied at 12:30 IST, which is later than the file mtime; the reason is not stated.
- **Upstream inputs.**
  - labels.parquet 0aefc00f425b (D via split_guard).
  - Runo call STT at ~/eta-data/forest/rl/stt: 933 D calls, Sarvam STT. Roles come from ft-engines roles_A (Nemotron overlap plus lexicon).
  - ft-calltext calls_D summaries.
  - ft-text2 Jev leak_union.
  - ft-audio features_ale: 198 consults, native Sarvam STT text, not Saaras English (FT6 ruling).
  - Bolna is a count only.
- **Split, cut-off and leakage.**
  - D only: all 1,233 episodes and 198 consults passed split_guard = D.
  - Call windows:
    - Transcripts: day < 14 (ft-engines elig_t14) and call day before the event.
    - Summaries: day <= 14 (ft-calltext).
  - Dropped items:
    - Items Haiku coded as outcome_revealed.
    - Untagged items.
    - ft-text2 Jev leak_union items (10 transcripts and 6 summaries).
  - Sensitivities:
    - Admin removed (pre-auth, insurance, payment and booking words).
    - Matched window (seed 20).
    - Day-3 landmark.
    - drop_decided.
  - Primary label Ale; h14 and h90 secondary.
- **Processing** (rebuild order prep, tag run (SCH=4 retry), jev_check run, lex, themes, sens_lex, other, quotes, report).
  - (1) Words: weighted log-odds with an informative Dirichlet prior (Monroe 2008), per corpus x speaker. An episode-level Fisher test goes into BH (family = label x mode).
  - (2) Themes:
    - 16 themes fixed in PLAN, coded one request per item. Every yes must have a code-verified verbatim quote.
    - Wilson CIs and Fisher tests. BH per mode, pooled over theme x corpus x label.
  - (3) Language and code-mixing (a script measure).
  - (4) Counsellor behaviour. There is no agent id in the D sources, so the per-counsellor table rests on names found in the text, with no tests run on it.
  - Total comparisons: 141,932.
  - Deviations from the plan are listed in the LEXICAL.md "Deviations" section.
- **Engines and model ids.**
  - Theme coding and snippet rendering: Haiku 5.5, `global.anthropic.claude-haiku-5-5`, via the Bedrock ZDR steward route (ft-calltext ftc_tag.client). Use: 4,804,976 in / 3,227,752 out tokens, 37 failed requests, 26 unverified quotes.
  - Cross-check: Jev `jev-latest`, 399 requests on a 15% sample (seed 20). Kappa reported per theme and per corpus.
  - Lexical statistics: scipy and pandas.
- **Checks.**
  - Per-theme kappa between Haiku and Jev.
  - The split_guard assert.
  - Refute ft-lexical-oref (Opus 5.5, 12:03): PASS-WITH-FIXES, fixes F1 to F8 applied per DONE.md. The refuter's recheck scripts are recheck_k, recheck_lex and recheck_window.

## Part 2. PROVENANCE (every number in FT7-REPORT.md and FT7-SUMMARY.md)

Generated by `scripts/provenance.py` (sha:c809d8baf726) from FT7-REPORT.md sha 64fbbb900428 and FT7-SUMMARY.md sha 2ae364ddcfa2.
- **"Traced" means mapped by its [Sx] tag to one source file, that file's producing script(s) and the file's sha256. It does not by itself mean the value was re-derived.** The VALUE-CHECK column adds a literal check: does the number string, as written (also tried without a leading +, without thousands commas, and with a unicode minus), occur in the cited file (YES/NO)? For a tag with several files, the row cites the FIRST file in tag order that holds the number (positional): its own sha and its own script(s). If no file holds it literally, the row says why when it can: a file value that rounds to it, or a fraction behind a percent. Small integers (for example 3, 7, 14) match almost any file, so a YES on them is weak evidence. Two further classes: META:date = a month range ("Aug-Sep 2026") whose source gives the range or both month names without the year; DERIVED = a number on a line marked "forest-lead's projection" that shows its arithmetic and is not itself in the source (the line's inputs that are in the source still count as YES).
- VALUE-CHECK counts: DERIVED 3; META:date 2; NO (explained: rounding/percent) 88; YES 1401.
- Traced numbers: 1494 (report 1410, summary 84). UNTRACED: 0. META (non-data) tokens: 74. Tokens in the Sources table and admin annex (metadata, not claims): 90.
- Mapping rules: see the script docstring; the `map` column says which rule resolved each row (line / parent-bullet / table-intro / table-caption / section-heading; summary rows: the report line they restate).
- `sha256[:12]` is recomputed now from the file on disk, not copied from the report (mismatches: see Source sha check).
